# S13 — Real-Provider Certification Matrix WorkGraph

**Slice:** S13 — Real-Provider Certification Matrix
**Branch:** `core-architecture-v2`
**Base:** `e3dad34` (S1–S12 independently accepted and committed; clean tree)
**Normative target:** `docs/CORE_ARCHITECTURE_V2.md` (unchanged by this slice)
**Mechanism classification:** `HYBRID`. Real model/provider journeys are `MODEL` executions; the certification oracle, workspace binding, evidence-lane classification, and matrix dispositions are `DETERMINISTIC`. A model receipt never becomes acceptance by itself: `CertificationCore` accepts only when the deterministic oracle passes, the packed candidate workspace still materializes the bound revision, and a required oracle-verified `model.evidence` check passed.

## Prerequisite verification (before implementation)

- `src/certification/{types,core,policy,oracle,bootstrap,codex,provider,adapters,artifacts,index}.ts` exist. `CERTIFICATION_CAPABILITY_MATRIX` in `src/certification/types.ts` contains the 22 capabilities named in the campaign.
- `runExternalSelfDogfood` packs the checkout, installs the artifact into a disposable fixture, binds `CandidateRevision` (packed artifact digest + workspace tree digest + source commit), and evaluates a deterministic `CertificationOracle`.
- `CodexAgentProvider` resolves `codex exec --help`, uses `-c model_reasoning_effort=...`, runs with a controlled temporary `HOME`/`CODEX_HOME` containing only the copied auth file, and records `executionEvidence` (started, provider, command, model, session id, exit code).
- Reconstructed environment facts (2026-09-26, verified, no secrets printed): `codex-cli 0.156.1` logged in via ChatGPT; `gh` authenticated as `JamesMorales04` (`repo`, `workflow`, `gist`, `read:org`); `paseo` 0.9.1 with a running local daemon; Paseo providers `codex` (available, `gpt-6-luna`) and `opencode` (available, catalog includes `opencode-go/mimo-v2.6-flash`); Playwright 1.62.1 + Chromium available; `bwrap` 0.13.0 available; node 22 / python 3.13; `podman`/`opa`/`cosign`/`trivy` absent (the S10 Trivy path `/tmp/opencode/s10-tools/trivy/0.70.0/trivy` no longer exists); npm registry reachable.
- Reconstructed certification-path defects (not from the campaign prompt):
  - `parsePackFilename` assumes `npm pack --json` stdout is pure JSON. With npm 10.9.8 `npm pack --ignore-scripts` still runs the package `prepare` script, whose build output precedes the JSON array, so the bootstrap fails with `npm pack did not return JSON.`
  - `runExternalSelfDogfood` records the harness build identity, but not the build identity of the freshly packed artifact (which may be a newer release produced by `prepare` during pack).
  - `createCommandOracle` emits only `fixture.command`/`model.evidence`; it cannot express per-capability `requiredEvidence` tokens, so `buildCapabilityResult` reports `INSUFFICIENT` for every capability lane.
  - The shipped default `workhorse` model alias `MiMo-V2.6-Flash` is absent from the local Paseo catalog (catalog id `mimo-v2.6-flash`), and the product `INTENT` semantic budget (`deadlineMs: 10_000`) is too short for any real Paseo-launched provider. Both are product findings, not fixture artifacts.

### Frozen matrix classification (22 rows)

Provider paths available in this environment are abbreviated below as:

- `PACKED_CODEX` — fresh packed artifact executed by the real `CodexAgentProvider` actor (`codex-cli 0.156.1`, `gpt-6-luna`); deterministic capability oracle verifies durable product artifacts.
- `PACKED_PASEO_MODEL` — packed product semantic runtime launched through the running Paseo daemon with a real local model (`opencode` runtime, `opencode-go/mimo-v2.6-flash`).
- `FULL_OPERATION` — a real packed governed operation (Paseo participants, candidate assembly, acceptance, delivery) is required; the harness does not exist yet and the shipped default `workhorse` alias names a model absent from the local catalog.
- `GITHUB_WRITE` — real public GitHub effects require an authorized disposable repository; none is authorized here.
- `BROWSER` — real Playwright Chromium lane; separately reported, never substituted for REAL_PROVIDER.

## Delegation Assessment

Writer units are **LEAD_OWNED**: the campaign forbids commits, so an isolated worktree cannot receive the lead's uncommitted implementation, and concurrent writers cannot be integrated without commits or shared-tree writes. The provider journeys themselves are bounded and sequential because they share the checkout `dist/` build output and the local Paseo daemon. A read-only independent verification lane may be delegated after the campaign if the reviewer wants a second provider receipt; workers never delegate further, never commit/push, and never touch `docs/CORE_ARCHITECTURE_V2.md`.

## Evidence lane plan

- CONTRACT/UNIT: bootstrap pack parsing, packed build identity binding, capability journey oracle checks, capability `requiredEvidence`/`model.evidence` semantics.
- REAL_PROVIDER: real `codex-cli 0.156.1` actor executions against a freshly packed candidate; real Paseo daemon + real local model session for the packed `intent` attempt.
- PACKED_E2E: every executed lane installs and runs the freshly packed artifact inside a disposable fixture.
- SYSTEM_DETERMINISTIC: deterministic oracle verification of durable fixture artifacts; checkout-untouched digest proof.
- BROWSER / ADVERSARIAL: not produced by S13; S11 BROWSER/VISUAL and S10 ADVERSARIAL evidence remain separately reported and are not substituted for REAL_PROVIDER.

## Execution record — round 4 (repair + re-run, 2026-09-27)

**Campaign result: `SLICE_BLOCKED` — repairs applied and exercised against real packed candidates, but no governed FULL_OPERATION row reached a real accepted terminal state before the round budget stop; `cancel`/`recovery`/`authority` remain as accepted in round 3, `issue-driven` remains PARTIAL, and `delivery` remains blocked.**

Round 4 repaired the round-3 FULL_OPERATION blockers and then uncovered four further real defects in the same path. Each repair has a focused regression. The durable evidence supports only this much: the packed change lane reached real ROUTE/STACK/CANDIDATE_IMPACT assessments and candidate r2 binding, then FAILED at the implementation stage with 0 terminal and 0 candidate-bound participant receipts, an unmutated candidate source digest, and no validation/acceptance artifact; the audit lane reached supervisor initialization, real reviewer sessions, validation and consolidation, then stalled before a terminal report. No implementer receipt, assembled ChangeSet or accepted terminal state exists in the round-4 artifacts.

The round completed eight packed-lane iterations plus focused validation. Per the Campaign Director's budget rule ("if approaching the cap, stop and report with per-row status rather than exceeding it"), the implementing lead stopped further lane iterations after the audit stall and is reporting per-row status instead of risking the ~1.2M-token round cap. ### Round-4 independent review (fresh reviewer, 2026-09-27): `SLICE_BLOCKED`

A fresh independent reviewer audited the round-4 candidate from source and evidence (machine record: `docs/evidence/s13/review-round4.json`). Branch/HEAD verified: `core-architecture-v2` at `e3dad34aa79348e1d65cee28114d76d4ec0a736c` (S12 acceptance), entire S13 uncommitted; `git diff --check` clean; TARGET `docs/CORE_ARCHITECTURE_V2.md` untouched; every documented evidence digest recomputed and matched its file. Focused verification re-ran 12 files / 84 tests green and `npm run typecheck` green.

Accepted-row dispositions (independently derived; a prior ACCEPTED label was not inherited): `startup`, `project-home`, `multi-project` — `UNCHANGED_EVIDENCE_VALID` (their CLI-only lanes do not execute any round-4 changed module); `cancel` and `recovery` — `UNCHANGED_EVIDENCE_VALID` (controller/lease/Paseo inspect-stop paths unchanged; the label-gated `runtimeCore` exemption cannot execute in those lanes); `authority` — `UNCHANGED_EVIDENCE_VALID` (gated-effect path unchanged; `git.ts` behavior is equivalent for fixtures without provider scratch); `informational` — `STALE_REQUIRES_RERUN` (round-4 semantic-assessor/paseo changes are directly on its real path); `issue-driven` — `BLOCKED` (PARTIAL; AEH-V2-0076); `control-center` — `NOT_APPLICABLE`; all other `FULL_OPERATION` rows — `BLOCKED`. All accepted rows still bind packed artifacts built before the round-4 edits, so a final build-bound sweep of accepted rows remains mandatory before any S13 checkpoint.

Review findings the round-5 lead must own:

- **R4-F2 (P0, truthfulness):** the durable `governed-lanes/change-direct.json` records `status=FAILED`, 0 terminal and 0 candidate-bound participant receipts, candidate `r2` source digest identical to the fixture tree digest, and no validation/acceptance artifact. The round-4 prose claiming "a real implementer receipt, isolated mutation and ChangeSet assembly" (this file, round-4 lane table and summary; STATUS round-4 paragraph; CONFORMANCE round-4 paragraph; ledger rows 0079/0080) is not supported by the artifact, and the lane-table attribution of the residual failure to AEH-V2-0081 is wrong (residual is AEH-V2-0083). This file's repair table also mis-numbers AEH-V2-0078/0079/0080/0081 relative to the ledger (table says 0078=workspace leak, 0079=artifact root, 0080=scratch, 0081=assessor; the ledger and STATUS correctly say 0078=continuation, 0079=workspace, 0080=artifact root, 0081=scratch, 0083=assessor). The ledger's AEH-V2-0082 "exact trace (`operation.watchdog.supervisor` success followed by repeated `no-lead stalled`)" is not present in `audit.json`; the trace was not preserved. Round 5 must correct all of these to match evidence.
- **R4-F3 (P0, product):** `finalizeOperationSession` still resolves operation-state reads/writes through the execution `root` (`loadOperation`, `registerOperationAgent`, `updateOperationParticipant`, `recordParticipantReceipt`) while only artifact paths use `operationArtifactRoot`; isolated DIRECT runs therefore cannot persist receipts (most calls are silently caught). AEH-V2-0080 is incomplete and plausibly explains the missing implementer receipt. Required: route all operation-state access in finalization through the authoritative state root and add an isolated-receipt regression.
- **R4-F1 (P1, regression coverage for AEH-V2-0075/0081):** the exclusion itself is verified safe — explicit, path-scoped to `.serena`/`graphify-out`, deterministic, untracked-only; an independent reproduction confirmed a newly created legitimate untracked file (`src/new.ts`) still enters the DIRECT ChangeSet while scratch is excluded, and arbitrary untracked files still enter the digest. No P0-A regression exists. But the reviewer-mandated regressions are missing: legitimate new untracked file perturbs identity; legitimate new untracked file appears in DIRECT ChangeSet; unknown untracked files are not silently ignored. Also make the filter intent-to-add-aware (staged intent-to-add entries surface under `git ls-files --cached`) or bind it to `generatedArtifactPaths(config)`.
- **R4-F4 (P0, AEH-V2-0082 diagnosis unverified):** the preserved evidence shows a single ACTIVE supervisor generation, no DRAINING generation, no watchdog trace, consolidation stage `COMPLETED`, operation `RUNNING`/revision 50 at the lane bound, and no report. The reported mechanism is a hypothesis. Round 5 must reproduce with `S13_GOV_KEEP=1` plus per-stage timestamps and `operation.watchdog.*` trace capture, then classify among (a) post-consolidation rotation/draining hang, (b) controller phase machine not advancing (`queued`/`preparing-audit`/`context` left RUNNING), (c) watchdog wake against an in-flight/draining generation, (d) report persisted but `result.taskId` never bound (the oracle reads the report through `result.taskId`), repair the owning defect, and require a terminal audit report.
- **R4-F5 (P0, AEH-V2-0076 confirmed cause):** `src/cli.ts` `issue import` → `src/issues/intake.ts` `normalizeIssueWithPlanner` → `executeAgentPrompt(..., requireExecutionAuthority: true)` → `src/security/executionLease.ts:53` `V2_AUTHORITY_REQUIRED` with no managed operation context. Required repair: route issue import through the managed controller authority path (controller-owned operation + candidate + epoch, Planner dispatched as a bounded participant with controller-issued authority, normalization receipt persisted). No `requireExecutionAuthority:false`, no ambient authority, no GitHub exception.
- **R4-F6 (P1, completion/retry hardening):** timeout retry trigger is a message regex rather than a typed classification; `extractLastAssistantText` can collect nested tool-payload strings instead of the canonical last assistant message, and `direction:"tail"` is unverified against the real SDK; `parseSemanticAssessmentOutputV1` brace fallback stays schema-gated. Hardening, not more retries.
- **R4-F7 (P1, AEH-V2-0079 guard):** `withinOperationWorkspace = !durableWorkspaceRoot || …` is fail-open when the durable operation cannot be loaded; require positive proof before claiming the operation workspace and canonicalize the root comparison.
- **R4-F8/F9 (P2):** the campaign summary still stamps `round: 3` and is overwritten per invocation (documented; per-lane artifacts are authoritative); `trivy`/`opengrep`/`opa`/`cosign` are declared optional validation-profile tools provisioned through the supported `aeh setup` path (classification A/B), `podman` is a system tool (C), `buildah` is undeclared and unrequired (E) — no current S13 row needs them; keep fail-closed and do not install substitutes.

Round-5 priority order: P0-A the required untracked-identity regressions and filter hardening (no product regression exists); P0-B AEH-V2-0082 reproduce → repair → terminal audit; P0-C AEH-V2-0076 is the managed authority path; P0-D AEH-V2-0083 residual + the R4-F3 receipt/state-root fix required for a terminal change/direct-change operation; then re-run audit and change/direct-change and unlock the dependent rows. Correctness before lane repetition; one bounded real-provider reproduction per repair; no acceptance-by-coercion; no retry inflation.

### Round-6 amendment — structured-output root cause (2026-09-27)

The provisional `REAL_PROVIDER_CONTRACT_LIMITATION` was withdrawn after a bounded real-channel probe established the actual failure boundary (`docs/evidence/s13/structured-output-probe.json`, SHA-256 `a72260fb2b1fe9fb318c0a354e62533467cb2fb9b6ae5d1f2fe76904709cae6b`).

**Channel trace.** `@getpaseo/server@0.9.1` receives `outputSchema` on the create request and passes `format: {type:"json_schema",schema}` to `session.promptAsync`; OpenCode 1.18.32 implements structured output as a required `StructuredOutput` tool (inputSchema = the supplied schema, `onSuccess` captures `info.structured`, `finish: "tool-calls"`), hardcodes `StructuredOutputError.retries = 0`, and does not itself validate tool arguments. When the tool is not called, Paseo emits only the raw text parts, so AEH never sees `StructuredOutputError`; AEH consumes the canonical object exactly when the provider produces it (probe trials with `info.structured` had zero text parts). The exact AEH layers are `parseSemanticAssessmentOutputV1` (unparseable) and the service payload schema/`validateAssessmentPayload` (schema-invalid); retries remain bounded at two with a single owner.

**Two owning AEH defects.** (1) `AEH_PROVIDER_ADAPTER_DEFECT`: the Semantic Assessor permission projection set `permission["*"]="deny"`, which also suppressed the injected `StructuredOutput` tool — the permission probe shows wildcard-deny fails, wildcard-deny + `StructuredOutput:"allow"` succeeds canonically, lowercase keys fail (case-sensitive), and explicit tool-family denies also succeed. The AEH-mediated assessor turn stalled until its 300 s deadline. (2) `AEH_STRUCTURED_OUTPUT_CAPABILITY_DEFECT`: runtime-level `structuredOutput=true` was treated as model proof while the assessor shipped on `opencode-go/mimo-v2.6-flash`, which is not certified for schema-bound output even with the channel open (3/3 full-schema trials captured `info.structured` with `judgment` as an encoded string). Contributing `AEH_PROMPT_SCHEMA_DEFECT`: the probe shows discipline materially improves tool-call compliance; the canonical discipline is now in the assessor prompt builder as error reduction only (never an authority). Not `AEH_COMPLETION_EXTRACTION_DEFECT`.

**Repairs.** Explicit `StructuredOutput: "allow"` under the retained repository/tool denials; a deterministic certified capability registry with canonical levels (`TEXTUAL_JSON < JSON_OBJECT < SCHEMA_BOUND_TOOL_CALL < JSON_SCHEMA_VALIDATED < JSON_SCHEMA_CONSTRAINED`), required level `SCHEMA_BOUND_TOOL_CALL`, probe-verified certified models (`opencode-go/gpt-6-luna`, `deepseek-v4.1-flash`, `kimi-k3`, `glm-5.3`), the shipped assessor default switched to `@structured-assessor` = `opencode-go/gpt-6-luna`, and fail-closed resolution that refuses uncertified models without silent fallback; the canonical output discipline in `SEMANTIC_ASSESSOR_SYSTEM_PROMPT`. Regressions: `tests/semanticStructuredOutputCapability.test.ts` (levels, registry, fail-closed selection, permission projection, discipline), plus the extended parse regression that an interior stray brace stays invalid (only trailing stray braces with no further JSON structure are recovered — syntax-preserving and non-semantic).

**Real verification after the repair.** AEH-mediated assessor probe: PASS in 11.3 s with model `opencode-go/gpt-6-luna`, typed STACK judgment, persisted assessment digest `5afdd632fff221910ad7fef4d2b1a17098d1826e9f598d2251f184f112687eee`, real Paseo session `51014e17-eb9d-4fce-9b96-0981079dfe5c` (container-bearing `workspaceId`). One bounded change/direct-change rerun then reached: real implementation, configured validation PASS, `evidence.requirement-coverage` PASS, a real independent Reviewer with `candidate.assurance.reviewer.0.reviewer` PASS, review `ACCEPTED/CONVERGED`, then terminal `FAILED` at deterministic AcceptanceOracle wiring — `VERIFICATION_VALIDATION_PATH_MISSING: assertion 'AC-1' has no resolved candidate-bound validation requirement` and `VERIFICATION_REVIEW_STRENGTH_INSUFFICIENT` because `currentReviewEvidence` requires the execution binding and the result artifact on one participant record while finalization stores the binding on the controller-issued authority identity and the artifact on the runtime session. The run exceeded the 900 s campaign lane bound (status `RUNNING` at capture, cancel attempt failed; it terminalized later and the durable record is preserved as `governed-lanes/change-direct-late-terminal.json`). These are the next deterministic blockers (`AEH-V2-0099`/`AEH-V2-0100`), not structured-output or model defects, and no gate was weakened. `mimo-v2.6-flash` remains documented as an uncertified model for semantic assessment; the pipeline is no longer classified as a real-provider contract limitation.

### AEH-V2-0106 — newly discovered downstream failure (round-8 owner)

The final run's AcceptanceOracle `ACCEPTED` and objective completion then failed closed on `PARTICIPANT_INCOMPLETE` for the implementer's controller-issued identity: its receipt binds candidate r2 (the candidate current during the isolated EXECUTING turn), while candidate r3 is the assembled result of that turn's ChangeSet and is the current candidate. The same equality is enforced by `evaluateTerminalGate` (`CANDIDATE_MISMATCH`), so even a passed objective completion would fail the successful-terminal gate. Reproduction: `tests/objectiveParticipantAccounting.test.ts` "documents the AEH-V2-0106 assembly-lineage gap" (receipt bound to the pre-assembly candidate closes neither the participant nor the terminal gate). This is a producer/evidence-model gap, not an AcceptanceOracle defect: TARGET requires the completion gate to evaluate compatible candidate/execution identities and assembly to be the only candidate-advancing transition, so the candidate binding of the work's receipt must be reconciled with the assembled candidate it produced (assembly-aware receipt binding) or the completion evidence must verify the deterministic assembly lineage. No gate was weakened and no evidence was re-bound in round 7; the repair belongs to round 8 with its own focused regressions.

### AEH-V2-0106 — deterministic assembly lineage (accepted in a real packed lane)

Every successful candidate transition now records a `CandidateAssemblyReceiptV1` at the ASSEMBLING boundary (`bindAssembledCandidate`; TARGET 8.1 "ChangeSet lineage and assembly receipt"): frozen base candidate, assembled candidate, ChangeSet/patch digests, ChangeSet producer identity, and — when a settled `SUCCEEDED` source receipt exists bound to the candidate the producer observed — that receipt's exact id and canonical digest (explicit wave rebases record the observed source base via `derivation`). `resolveCandidateLineageReceiptV1` walks the durable chain from the current candidate and accepts a work receipt only when the chain names that exact receipt id/digest and the receipt's observed candidate lies on the walked ancestry; a broken chain, a divergent source candidate, a tampered assembly digest, an unsettled source receipt or an unrelated receipt stays rejected. `objectiveParticipantAccountingV1` (moved to `src/operations/participantAccounting.ts` so producer and verifier share one deterministic derivation) and the successful-terminal receipt decision consume the resolver; `evaluateTerminalGate` is byte-identical and is still invoked against each receipt's own binding, so direct current-bound receipts behave exactly as before. The completion snapshot check recomputes the same accounting, so a fabricated `COMPLETED` snapshot is rejected. AEH-V2-0089 resumability is preserved: the launch identity stays non-terminal and closes `COMPLETED` only through its settled lineage receipt; the receipt is never rewritten and the artifact chain remains coherent.

### AEH-V2-0107 — FORMAL_SDD escalation policy rebind

The first `change-delegated` run routed FORMAL_SDD from durable explorer/planner evidence and failed at the Spec Manager launch with `EXECUTION_POLICY_STALE` (intent `FORMAL_SDD`/`ELEVATED`, frozen policy `DELEGATED`/`NONE`). `rebindEscalatedChangePolicy` now advances the operation execution revision (establishing the pre-escalation semantics baseline first when no digest exists), clears the superseded policy through the canonical `bindOperationExecutionSemantics`, and rebinds a recompiled policy with the escalated route/assurance for the current candidate and epoch before any FORMAL_SDD participant launches; repeating the identical escalation is idempotent. No policy guard or gate was weakened.

### AEH-V2-0114 repair (P0)

`tests/packed/s13ExternalEffectAuthorization.mjs` is the only producer of `ACTION_AUTHORIZATION` approvals for the GitHub campaign. It loads the user authorization file from `S13_GH_AUTHORIZATION_FILE` and validates, before any external command or staging directory exists: `kind=USER_EXTERNAL_EFFECT_AUTHORIZATION`, `authorizedBy=user`, active status, `scope.owner`, `scope.privacy=PRIVATE_ONLY`, a non-empty machine-readable `scope.allowedEffects`, `scope.resourceNamePrefix`, `governance.ambientAuthorityGranted=false`, `governance.campaignSelfAuthorization` prohibiting campaign minting, and `consumption.requiredBeforeAnyExternalCommand=true`. It fails with typed errors (`EXTERNAL_AUTHORIZATION_MISSING`, `_UNREADABLE`, `_INVALID`, `_OUT_OF_SCOPE`, `_EFFECT_NOT_COVERED`, `_POLICY_NOT_BOUND`, `_NOT_VALIDATED`) and returns an unforgeable validated object (a private `Symbol` brand). `externalAuthorizationDecisionInputV1` derives the only `APPROVE` from that object with `actorId: human:external-authorization:<authorizationId>` and a reason binding the artifact sha256 and the exact effect digest; the campaign also asserts coverage against the frozen `allowedExternalEffects` and the `aeh-s13-` resource namespace. The default lane reuses the user-authorized existing issue #1; governed issue creation requires explicit `github.issue.create` coverage. Repository creation/deletion is not hardcoded as banned: it is simply not covered by the current authorization artifact, so any such request fails closed on the same generic path; a future authorization can cover additional effects. Issues #2/#3 were not edited or deleted and deletion was not retried.

**Prospective authorization amendment.** `docs/evidence/s13/github-effect-authorization.json` gained machine-readable `scope.resourceNamePrefix: "aeh-s13-"` and `scope.allowedEffects: ["git.push","github.branch.create","github.issue.create"]` with an `amendments` record citing the previous sha256 `31a4743754d27e2fd45e8df05316691052f14d7f3e4e5e97a624a7ff15249b49`. This encodes the same prospective user grant and does not retroactively validate Round-10 effects; repository create/delete and issue edit/delete remain uncovered. Current digest `272c5cb76158a7c312edd4d8d97cd6545b599e5c57c7978bbfc3742c573bd460`.

### Round-12 repairs with regressions

- **AEH-V2-0115 (P0-A).** `src/spec/openspec.ts` validates every READY spec delta before any controller-owned write: canonical kebab-case capability, at least one delta section (`## ADDED/MODIFIED/REMOVED/RENAMED Requirements`), every `### Requirement:` inside a delta, and at least one `#### Scenario:` per requirement; duplicate capabilities and flat requirement documents fail with `SPEC_MANAGER_CONTENT_NOT_CANONICAL` naming `openspec/changes/<change>/specs/<capability>/spec.md (artifacts.specs[i])`. `src/agents/outputContracts.ts` and the Spec Manager prompt now require the canonical `{ capability, content }` shape. Regressions: `tests/specManagerCanonicality.test.ts` (6; includes a frozen-controller run proving the failure happens before persistence and before `spec-compilation`, and an escalation-path regression). The Round-11 flat `### Requirement:` document is rejected; structurally non-canonical or empty content is rejected before persistence (the gate validates canonical structure, not semantic content quality, which remains subject to `openspec validate --strict`).
- **AEH-V2-0110 (P0-B).** Escalation is now `PLANNER_FORMALIZATION_NEED_REQUIRED` only. The event carries the constraint, Planner artifact, `formalizationReason`, `formalizationEvidenceRefs` and Explorer finding statuses. Regressions: `tests/changeRouting.test.ts` (PARTIAL Explorer finding + `formalizationNeed: NONE` cannot escalate). The live Round-12 lane `run-r12-delegated-2` shows exactly one legitimate Planner-REQUIRED escalation with full provenance; runs 4–11 show zero escalation events with the refactor fixture.
- **Pipeline defects found by the live lane (each fixed with a focused regression).**
  1. Planning ran before the TaskContract existed, so no plan could reference the frozen requirement ids (`run-r12-delegated-1`). Fix: DELEGATED seals its routed contract before `planAgainstSealedContract`; FORMAL_SDD formalizes and compiles before planning (TARGET 4.1/4.2). Regression: `tests/changePlanRequirementTraceability.test.ts` (contract sealed before planning, Planner references `AC-1`).
  2. A Planner-driven escalation skipped the formalization branch (the pre-escalation `else if` chain) and ran the formal route through implementation (`EXECUTION_POLICY_STALE`, `run-r12-delegated-2`). Fix: the DIRECT/FORMAL branch is evaluated after the escalation and a missing contract fails with `CHANGE_CONTRACT_MISSING`. Regression: `tests/specManagerCanonicality.test.ts` escalation-path test.
  3. The first real planning semantics bind collided with the provisional bootstrap policy (`EXECUTION_POLICY_RECOMPILE_REQUIRED`; `run-r12-delegated-7`). Fix: `isProvisionalOperationPolicyV1` treats the bootstrap policy as provisional; the first execution-semantics bind supersedes it exactly once (revision +1, policy cleared, bindings invalidated), and the transition guards allow exactly that. Regression: `tests/specEscalationPolicyRebind.test.ts` provisional-supersede test.
  4. A known technology competency (`node-runtime`) was simultaneously "known" and "uncovered" because technology skills were selected only by specialization (`run-r12-delegated-6`). Fix: a requested competency selects the seed skill that defines it. Regression: `tests/participantsCanonical.test.ts`.
  5. The compiled assignment tool ceiling did not project explicit launch permissions, so a real OpenCode Implementer session ran with provider-default "ask" (`run-r12-delegated-8` silently completed with an empty result). Fix: `selectionForParticipant` projects read/write/gitWrite/shell/network explicitly from the ceiling-checked toolPack, never widening an explicit base deny or operator `ask`. Regression: `tests/participantRuntimeBinding.test.ts`.
  6. A `command`-kind validation requirement that does not name an approved command id blocked the plan (`run-r12-delegated-9`). Fix: exactly one configured command is the deterministic resolution, matching the existing single-validator/provider rule. Regression: `tests/architectureWorkGraph.test.ts`.
  7. A provider session stopped on its approval prompt was treated as a successful turn (exit 0, empty output) and only surfaced as an empty structured result (`run-r12-delegated-8`). Fix: `permission`/`waiting` are terminal failures with an explicit stderr detail; `featureValues` is plumbed through the launch spec and SDK create options, and `auto_accept` is enabled only when the projected permissions contain no operator `ask`. Regressions: `tests/paseoRuntime.test.ts`, `tests/paseoLaunchSpec.test.ts`.

### AEH-V2-0116 diagnosis and repairs (P0-A)

Full machine record: `docs/evidence/s13/round13-0116-diagnosis.json`. Classification: `AEH_PRODUCT_DEFECTS_REPAIRED_PLUS_PROVIDER_BEHAVIOR_DOCUMENTED`.

- **Provider behavior (documented, never waived).** (1) Paseo deterministically disables OpenCode `auto_accept` whenever `config.toolPolicy` is present (`@getpaseo/server@0.9.1` `opencode-agent.js:2425`), so `featureValues.auto_accept: true` alone can never clear a prompt on any AEH launch carrying MCP preapprovals. (2) Paseo child agents inherit the parent agent's workspace and cwd (probe6: a child created with `cwd=/tmp/...` and `parent=<workspace-bound agent>` was observed at the parent's worktree and workspace).
- **D1 (repaired).** The OpenCode projection omitted `external_directory` for the frozen launch root, so the session stopped at `provider session stopped on an unapproved 'permission' prompt (external_directory scope …)`. `src/agents/permissions.ts` now projects a scoped allow for the launch root and its git metadata roots (new `src/core/git.ts` `worktreeGitRoots`), only for roles with read/write/shell authority; regressions in `tests/permissions.test.ts` and `tests/paseoLaunchSpec.test.ts`; directive probes reproduced the stop and proved the scoped forms clear it.
- **D2 (repaired).** The SDK discarded `WaitForFinishResult.final.pendingPermissions`; `src/paseo/sdk.ts` + `src/paseo/native.ts` now capture a bounded permission identity and `src/paseo/runtimeCore.ts` includes it in the failed-turn stderr (regression `tests/paseoRuntime.test.ts`). The next live lane's failure detail named the exact permission and scope.
- **D3 (repaired).** Codex participants ran with the provider-default `on-request` approval policy and stopped on `CodexBash`/`CodexFileChange`; `src/paseo/launchSpec.ts` now projects `providerOptions` from the compiled ceiling (`approval_policy: never`, `sandbox_mode: workspace-write|read-only`, `sandbox_workspace_write` writable roots + network from the ceiling) through `src/paseo/sdk.ts`; CLI fallback is refused when provider options exist (fail closed).
- **D4 → AEH-V2-0117 (repaired).** Isolated wave-task launches carried the supervisor as the Paseo `parent`; Paseo relocated the session to the candidate workspace, which the Implementer then mutated (watcher: `src/format.mjs` added in the candidate worktree) and `recordParticipantReceipt` failed with `CANDIDATE_WORKSPACE_MISMATCH`. `src/paseo/launchSpec.ts` now omits the Paseo parent handle for launches whose frozen root differs from the durable workspace root while keeping `aeh.parent-agent`/`AEH_PARENT_AGENT_ID` correlation (regression in `tests/paseoLaunchSpec.test.ts`).

No Repairer runs, no PASS is coerced, and no gate is weakened. The Round-14 remediation budget remains as a deterministic bound for genuine non-converging semantic debt. The reviewer prompt now states the authorized read boundary and the changed files/requirements, reducing out-of-scope provider stops. | `tests/reviewRepairCandidateLifecycle.test.ts` (5: reviewer provider stop → single reviewer turn, no Repairer, typed check; mutation attempt → same; SYSTEM_FAILURE-typed finding → terminal; quality state present; review remediation lifecycle), `tests/reviewLifecycle.test.ts` (budget + failure-detail), `tests/paseoLaunchSpec.test.ts` control-root external scope |

| Defect | Resolution | Regression |

| **AEH-V2-0123** — resolved base (plan-authored) validation requirements were compiled into the candidate assurance and verification requirements but never executed into report checks, so the AcceptanceOracle saw dangling `architecture-boundary`/`fixture-greeting` requirements and rejected with `VERIFICATION_VALIDATION_EVIDENCE_INSUFFICIENT`; the Lead prompt also did not map assertions to their resolved validation evidence. | `runCandidateImpactValidations` accepts an explicit requirement set; the assurance recompilation executes every resolved base requirement that has no matching report check (check-id or `details.requirementId`), reusing already-executed configured checks, and the replaced messages name the requirement generically. The Lead prompt now projects `validationRequirement(kind)=oracleCheckStatus` per assertion. No gate weakened: the oracle's exact-one-check rule and the deterministic schema/coverage checks are unchanged. | `tests/acceptanceValidationPath.test.ts` (executes an unmatched resolved plan requirement and proves the oracle accepts exactly one check), `tests/managedLeadAcceptance.test.ts` (resolved assertion evidence in the prompt) |
| **AEH-V2-0124** — a settled receipt for non-producing bounded work (Explorer/Planner/Reviewer) bound to an ancestor candidate of the verified assembly chain was not completion evidence, and a resumable launch identity's transient live `FAILED` from a retried attempt shadowed its later settled receipt; objective completion and the terminal gate both failed `PARTICIPANT_INCOMPLETE`. | `resolveCandidateLineageReceiptV1` returns the new `ANCESTOR` resolution for settled `SUCCEEDED` receipts of known non-producing roles (`Explorer`, `Planner`, `Reviewer`, `Librarian`, `Spec Manager`) whose bound candidate is on the deterministic assembly ancestry; unknown/absent roles and producers (`Implementer`, `Repairer`) stay strict and must be assembly-named. `objectiveParticipantAccountingV1` prefers the lineage-proven settled receipt over a transient live status, and `terminalReceiptDecision` evaluates an `ANCESTOR` receipt against the candidate the work observed (the terminal gate itself is unchanged). | `tests/objectiveParticipantAccounting.test.ts` (ancestor completion for a live-FAILED Explorer identity; producer receipt not named by the assembly stays rejected; terminal-gate basis), `tests/assemblyLineageCompletion.test.ts` |

### Round-17 product change and re-freeze (`5d1b6680…`)

**Product change (P0-D, requested effects enumerated in frozen policy — TARGET 11):** `delivery.github.pullRequests?: boolean` (default `true`) added to the project config, types and schema. When `false`, `configuredExternalEffects` omits `github.pull-request.create` (keeping `git.push`/`github.branch.create`/`github.issue.create`) and `finalizeAcceptedIssue` returns a push-only `FINALIZED` after the gated push without attempting a pull request. Focused regressions: `tests/deliveryFinalize.test.ts` (12 tests; push-only finalization with exactly the commit+push intents and no `/pulls` request) and `tests/security/actionPolicy.test.ts` (11 tests; push-only effect/requirement enumeration). This is required by the authorization chain: the user authorization covers `git.push`, `github.branch.create` and `github.issue.create` only, and no self-amended authorization was minted.

**Re-freeze:** trackedDigest `5d1b6680ee1d4f6b7ff623521ceedb77ae70e3f4180d2a48946e3e1c7adc4d7e` (668 files), build `release-1790684324214-110138-2fd31d7e`, `npm run typecheck` exit 0, `git diff --check` clean, target sha256 `4954b62b…`. It supersedes `2bee2ec0…` for every row whose executed path includes the changed modules (`src/core/{types,config}.ts`, `src/security/actionPolicy.ts`, `src/delivery/finalize.ts`, `schemas/project.schema.json`).

**Source-delta equivalence (rows not requesting push-only delivery):** the new field is optional and absent from every pre-existing fixture/config; `configuredExternalEffects` returns the identical sorted effect list, `finalizeAcceptedIssue` takes the identical code path, and config parsing accepts exactly the same documents. The changed behavior activates only when `pullRequests: false` is set, which only the Round-17 delivery lane does. **All current rows were re-run on the new freeze:** GitHub `issue-driven`/`authority`/`delivery` (`r17-gh-4`), `change-formal` (`r17-formal-3`), certification 4 (`r17-certification-2`), lifecycle `cancel`/`recovery` (`r17-lifecycle-2`), `change`/`direct-change` (`r17-direct-1`), `audit` (`r17-audit-2`), `delegated-change` (`r17-delegated-1`). ## Director amendment — CAMPAIGN RESOURCE-LIFECYCLE (2026-09-29, user-mandated)

A system-wide resource/process accumulation incident was observed during the long-running campaign. The following rules are now campaign law for every subsequent round and handoff, and they apply before creating the next round/lead.

1. **No idle resource accumulation.** After a lead/reviewer/worker's durable output is preserved and no live continuation depends on it: verify ownership → archive the agent via Paseo's supported archive lifecycle. IDLE is not released.
2. **Workspace reconciliation before each round.** Enumerate active campaign-owned agents and workspaces. Terminal operation + campaign-owned + no live continuation → archive. Live/nonterminal → preserve. Uncertain ownership → preserve and report. Never bulk-archive resources whose terminality is unknown; a campaign timeout is not operation terminality.
3. **Director stays thin.** Retain only the current Director, current Slice Lead, a current independent reviewer when needed, and explicitly live workers. Consume and archive previous-round leads/reviewers after handoff.
4. **Resource census at every round boundary** (compact; no credentials/environment): active agents, active workspaces, campaign provider/session count where observable, child-process count, cleanup/archive results. Monotonic growth across completed rounds is a product/runtime lifecycle defect, not a reason to continue.
5. **AEH-V2-0130 opened** (next correct ledger ID): investigate whether AEH can leave provider sessions, Paseo agents/workspaces, Serena/runtime processes or subordinates alive after the owning operation is terminal. Do not assume a Paseo heap leak; classify the owner from evidence. Required architecture: durable operation ownership → durable provider/resource ownership → terminal/recovery reconciliation → safe release/archive → verification.
6. **Recovery reconciliation.** Restart/recovery must distinguish live owned resource, terminal-operation orphan, and unknown/unowned resource. Only terminal-operation resources with proven AEH ownership may be auto-reclaimed; never garbage-collect unrelated user Paseo sessions.
7. **Resource-stability test required before final self-hosting acceptance:** a bounded repeated-operation campaign proving completed operations do not cause monotonic accumulation of active agents, workspaces, or provider sessions/processes; use trend/bounded-resource assertions (not byte-identical RSS).
8. **Backpressure:** if the implementation can spawn provider sessions without an operation/campaign resource ceiling, investigate policy-driven concurrency/resource backpressure. No machine-specific memory constants in Core.
9. **S13/S14:** do not waive current S13 certification requirements. Where this defect affects certified runtime/provider-lifecycle claims, determine the affected evidence and rerun only those lanes. S14's disposable self-hosting composite must include resource cleanup/reconciliation.

### AEH-V2-0130 investigation (W114, read-only source evidence; 2026-09-29T20:55Z)

Classification: `AEH_PRODUCT_DEFECT` — missing terminal/recovery resource reconciliation, **not** a Paseo heap leak. Source evidence (`e3dad34` + uncommitted S13 source; no source edited by this investigation):

- **Ownership data exists but is not reconciled at terminal.** `OperationRecordV2` durably carries `workspaceId`/`workspaceRoot` and per-participant `workspaceId`/`executionBinding.runtime.sessionId` (`src/operations/state.ts:142-147`). `terminalizeOperation` (`src/operations/controller.ts:787`) only patches terminal state + notification: no stop/archive/reconciliation of owned workspaces, agents or provider sessions.
- **Per-turn provider lifecycle is sound.** `runWithOperationProviderLease` (`src/runtime/providerLifecycle.ts:48`) acquires a durable lease, renews it, observes session quiescence via `stopAndObserveQuiescence`, releases the lease, and on failure either releases after quiescence or marks the lease uncertain (`markLeaseUncertain`). This is why no live provider process belonged to a *completed* turn; the Round-19 survivors were turns whose controller was killed mid-run.
- **Cancellation reconciliation is complete; normal terminal is not.** The CANCELLED path (`src/operations/controller.ts:640-730`) stops participant agents, inspects provider leases, stops non-quiescent sessions, releases leases, and refuses terminal status while writers are unfenced. No equivalent SUCCEEDED/FAILED path exists.
- **Supervisor generations are archived deterministically** (`settleDrainingSupervisorGenerations`, `src/operations/supervisor.ts:359-370`, `archivePaseoSdkAgent`); **participant/lead agent records are stopped but never archived**, which explains the census's 415 dormant campaign-owned store records. Operation workspaces (`paseo workspace create`, LOCAL_RESOURCE_CREATION) have no product-side archive path — cleanup lives only in campaign harnesses.
- **Recovery has no orphan reconciliation.** A controller killed mid-operation leaves its fixture child processes (`serena-pool`, `serena-proxy`, `resultSinkMcp`, `context mcp`; observed live in the running journey fixture at 20:57Z) and its Paseo agent/workspace records with no owner-side sweep; restart cannot distinguish live-owned / terminal-orphan / unknown from a durable record alone.

Required architecture (unchanged, from the amendment): durable operation ownership → durable provider/resource ownership → terminal/recovery reconciliation → safe release/archive → verification, with live-owned / terminal-orphan / unknown classification and no GC of unrelated user Paseo sessions. **Exact plan (handoff if capacity is exhausted):** (1) persist a per-operation resource ledger (`resource: { workspaceId, agentIds[], providerSessionIds[] }` written at creation/launch time); (2) extend the terminal transition to a deterministic `reconcileOperationResources` step that stops non-quiescent owned sessions, archives owned participant/lead agents, archives owned workspaces only after quiescence, and records `resourceReconciliation` on the operation; (3) add a recovery sweep that only reclaims resources whose owning operation is durably terminal; (4) add `tests/packed/s13ResourceStabilityCampaign.mjs` — repeated bounded operations with trend assertions (agent/workspace/provider-process counts per iteration; fail on monotonic growth beyond a declared ceiling); (5) S14 disposable self-hosting composite must include this cleanup/reconciliation step. A `src/**` repair here cuts a new freeze; affected runtime/provider-lifecycle lanes would then be re-run per P2-G.

### Round-21 step log (updated after every step)

- `2026-09-30T00:25Z` startup verification above; Round-21 record written before any lane work.
